# Command lines of xtask's command table and their outcomes, recorded from the
# clap 4.6 derive parser at 9c8b0c7a0, before the in-tree parser replaced it
# (xtask/src/argparse/reference.rs). The test
# `argparse::reference::reference_command_lines_parse_as_recorded` parses each
# command line and checks its outcome against the record;
# `XTASK_BLESS_REFERENCE=1 cargo xtask test` rewrites the outcomes from the
# current table.
#
# A record is the command line as a JSON array of the arguments after the
# program's name, then its outcome:
#   ok <command> <args>      the command parsed; each argument given on the
#                            command line, `id` for a flag, `id=[values]`
#                            otherwise
#   ok-all <command> <args>  as `ok`, and each argument holding its default
#                            value, `id=default[values]` (`id=default` for a
#                            flag); one record of each command that parses
#   help <screen>            the help screen of help.txt with that name
#   help-on-missing <screen> the same, printed for a missing subcommand
#   <ErrorKind>              the error's kind, then its message up to its
#                            first blank line (the usage and the tips after it
#                            are left out)
# Records are separated by a blank line.

[]
help-on-missing xtask -h

["-h"]
help xtask -h

["--help"]
help xtask -h

["help"]
help xtask -h

["help","help"]
help xtask help help
